2026-02-23 10:22:54 | Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command | INF | Executed DbCommand (8ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT f."FileId", (
    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')
    ORDER BY f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" DESC
    LIMIT 1), TIMESTAMPTZ '-infinity') AS "ModifiedTime"
FROM file_change_logs AS f
WHERE f."FileId" IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY f."FileId"

2026-02-23 14:04:33 | Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command | INF | Executed DbCommand (13ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT f."FileId", (
    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')
    ORDER BY f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" DESC
    LIMIT 1), TIMESTAMPTZ '-infinity') AS "ModifiedTime"
FROM file_change_logs AS f
WHERE f."FileId" IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY f."FileId"

2026-02-23 14:10:54 | Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command | INF | Executed DbCommand (4ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT f."FileId", (
    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')
    ORDER BY f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" DESC
    LIMIT 1), TIMESTAMPTZ '-infinity') AS "ModifiedTime"
FROM file_change_logs AS f
WHERE f."FileId" IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY f."FileId"

2026-02-23 14:19:50 | Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command | INF | Executed DbCommand (4ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT f."FileId", (
    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')
    ORDER BY f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" DESC
    LIMIT 1), TIMESTAMPTZ '-infinity') AS "ModifiedTime"
FROM file_change_logs AS f
WHERE f."FileId" IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY f."FileId"

2026-02-23 14:23:30 | Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command | INF | Executed DbCommand (5ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT f."FileId", (
    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')
    ORDER BY f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" DESC
    LIMIT 1), TIMESTAMPTZ '-infinity') AS "ModifiedTime"
FROM file_change_logs AS f
WHERE f."FileId" IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY f."FileId"

    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((

    FROM file_change_logs AS f1

2026-02-23 16:19:14 | Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command | INF | Executed DbCommand (5ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT f."FileId", (
    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')
    ORDER BY f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" DESC
    LIMIT 1), TIMESTAMPTZ '-infinity') AS "ModifiedTime"
FROM file_change_logs AS f
WHERE f."FileId" IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY f."FileId"

2026-02-23 17:03:00 | Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command | INF | Executed DbCommand (5ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT f."FileId", (
    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')
    ORDER BY f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" DESC
    LIMIT 1), TIMESTAMPTZ '-infinity') AS "ModifiedTime"
FROM file_change_logs AS f
WHERE f."FileId" IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY f."FileId"

2026-02-23 17:25:41 | Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command | INF | Executed DbCommand (4ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT f."FileId", (
    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')
    ORDER BY f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" DESC
    LIMIT 1), TIMESTAMPTZ '-infinity') AS "ModifiedTime"
FROM file_change_logs AS f
WHERE f."FileId" IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY f."FileId"

2026-02-23 17:31:00 | Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command | INF | Executed DbCommand (7ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT f."FileId", (
    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')
    ORDER BY f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" DESC
    LIMIT 1), TIMESTAMPTZ '-infinity') AS "ModifiedTime"
FROM file_change_logs AS f
WHERE f."FileId" IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY f."FileId"

2026-02-23 17:40:22 | Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Database.Command | INF | Executed DbCommand (6ms) [Parameters=[], CommandType='"Text"', CommandTimeout='30']
SELECT f."FileId", (
    SELECT f0."ChangeType"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f0
    WHERE f0."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f0."FileId"
    ORDER BY f0."DetectedAt" DESC
    LIMIT 1) AS "LatestChangeType", COALESCE((
    SELECT f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime"
    FROM file_change_logs AS f1
    WHERE f1."FileId" IS NOT NULL AND f."FileId" = f1."FileId" AND f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" IS NOT NULL AND f1."ChangeType" IN ('Upload', 'Modified')
    ORDER BY f1."GoogleDriveModifiedTime" DESC
    LIMIT 1), TIMESTAMPTZ '-infinity') AS "ModifiedTime"
FROM file_change_logs AS f
WHERE f."FileId" IS NOT NULL
GROUP BY f."FileId"
